{"forms":[{"form":"sissy squats","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["An isolation exercise for the quadriceps femoris in which, from a standing position, the knees bend forward while the hips remain extended and the lifter leans backwards. The knees do not need to touch the floor before returning to a standing position.
